function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function printQuestions() {
  if (!currentQuestions.length) return;
  const escapeHtml = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[char]));
  const renderQuestion = (q, index) => `<div class="q">
<div class="n">Q${index + 1}　${escapeHtml(q.id)}</div>
<div class="body">${escapeHtml(q.q)}</div>${showAnswers ? `<div class="a">答え: ${escapeHtml(q.a)}</div>` : '<div class="answer-space" aria-label="解答欄">
</div>'}</div>`;
  const pages = [];
  for (let startIndex = 0; startIndex < currentQuestions.length; startIndex += 10) {
    const pageQuestions = currentQuestions.slice(startIndex, startIndex + 10);
    pages.push({
      html: pageQuestions.map((q, offset) => renderQuestion(q, startIndex + offset)).join(''),
      rows: Math.ceil(pageQuestions.length / 2)
    });
  }
  const printHtml = `<!doctype html>
<html lang="ja">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<title>問題プリント</title>
<style>
  @page{size:A4 portrait;margin:8mm}
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI","Yu Gothic",sans-serif;margin:0;color:#111;font-size:9pt;line-height:1.3}
  .page{height:281mm;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;page-break-after:always;break-after:page}
  .page:last-child{page-

let currentQuestions = [];
let showAnswers = false;
const STORAGE_KEY = 'mikamijuku_problem_app_v3';

function captureState() {
  return {
    subject: el('subjectSelect')?.value || '英語',
    grade: el('gradeSelect')?.value || '中1',
    textbook: el('textbookSelect')?.value || '',
    section: el('sectionSelect')?.value || '',
    questionCount: el('questionCount')?.value || '10',
    vocabMode: el('vocabMode')?.value || 'on',
    pickMode: el('pickMode')?.value || 'random',
    stages: selectedValues('input[data-stage]'),
    types: selectedValues('input[data-type]'),
    listSubject: el('listSubject')?.value || '英語',
    listGrade: el('listGrade')?.value || 'all',
    listLimit: el('listLimit')?.value || '50',
    listKeyword: el('listKeyword')?.value || '',
    listVocabMode: el('listVocabMode')?.value || 'same',
    currentQuestionIds: currentQuestions.map(q => q.id),
    showAnswers
  };
}

function saveState(silent = true) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(captureState()));
    if (!silent && el('globalSaveStatus')) {
      el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = 'この端末に保存しました。';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存に失敗しました。';
  }
}

function restoreQuestionSetByIds(ids) {
  const map = new Map(qb.map(q => [q.id, q]));
  const restored = (ids || []).map(id => map.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (restored.length) {
    currentQuestions = restored;
    el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = restored.map((q, i) => `
      <div class="q">
        <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
        <div>${q.q}</div>
        <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    el('builderStatus').textContent = '保存されていた問題セットを復元しました。';
    el('builderStatus').classList.add('good');
  }
}

function safeSetValue(id, value) {
  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;
  const has = Array.from(node.options || []).some(op => op.value === value || op.textContent === value);
  if (has) node.value = value;
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);
  renderSectionOptions();
  renderStageOptions();
  renderTypeOptions();
  safeSetValue('textbookSelect', state.textbook);
  renderSectionOptions();
  safeSetValue('sectionSelect', state.section);
  safeSetValue('questionCount', state.questionCount);
  safeSetValue('vocabMode', state.vocabMode);
  safeSetValue('pickMode', state.pickMode);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-stage]', state.stages);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-type]', state.types);
  syncListSelectors();
  safeSetValue('listSubject', state.listSubject);
  safeSetValue('listGrade', state.listGrade);
  safeSetValue('listLimit', state.listLimit);
  safeSetValue('listVocabMode', state.listVocabMode);
  if (el('listKeyword')) el('listKeyword').value = state.listKeyword || '';
  showAnswers = !!state.showAnswers;
  restoreQuestionSetByIds(state.currentQuestionIds || []);
  updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
}

function exportStateFile() {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(captureState(), null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = 'mikamijuku_app_state_latest.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 3000);
  if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを書き出しました。';
}

function importStateFile(file) {
  const fr = new FileReader();
  fr.onload = () => {
    try {
      const state = JSON.parse(fr.result);
      applyState(state);
      saveState(true);
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを読み込みました。';
    } catch (e) {
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データの読み込みに失敗しました。';
    }
  };
  fr.readAsText(file, 'utf-8');
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);
  renderSectionOptions();
  renderStageOptions();
  renderTypeOptions();
  safeSetValue('textbookSelect', state.textbook);
  renderSectionOptions();
  safeSetValue('sectionSelect', state.section);
  safeSetValue('questionCount', state.questionCount);
  safeSetValue('vocabMode', state.vocabMode);
  safeSetValue('pickMode', state.pickMode);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-stage]', state.stages);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-type]', state.types);
  syncListSelectors();
  safeSetValue('listSubject', state.listSubject);
  safeSetValue('listGrade', state.listGrade);
  safeSetValue('listLimit', state.listLimit);
  safeSetValue('listVocabMode', state.listVocabMode);
  if (el('listKeyword')) el('listKeyword').value = state.listKeyword || '';
  showAnswers = !!state.showAnswers;
  restoreQuestionSetByIds(state.currentQuestionIds || []);
  updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);
  renderSectionOptions();
  renderStageOptions();
  renderTypeOptions();
  safeSetValue('textbookSelect', state.textbook);
  renderSectionOptions();
  safeSetValue('sectionSelect', state.section);
  safeSetValue('questionCount', state.questionCount);
  safeSetValue('vocabMode', state.vocabMode);
  safeSetValue('pickMode', state.pickMode);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-stage]', state.stages);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-type]', state.types);
  syncListSelectors();
  safeSetValue('listSubject', state.listSubject);
  safeSetValue('listGrade', state.listGrade);
  safeSetValue('listLimit', state.listLimit);
  safeSetValue('listVocabMode', state.listVocabMode);
  if (el('listKeyword')) el('listKeyword').value = state.listKeyword || '';
  showAnswers = !!state.showAnswers;
  restoreQuestionSetByIds(state.currentQuestionIds || []);
  updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
}

function renderTabs() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const target = document.getElementById('screen-' + btn.dataset.screen);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
}

function renderTextbookOptions() {
  el('textbookSelect').innerHTML = meta.textbooks.map(tb => `<option>${tb}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderSectionOptions() {
  const subject = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade().replace('中','');
  if (subject !== '英語') {
    el('textbookWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    el('sectionWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  el('textbookWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  el('sectionWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  const tb = currentTextbook() || meta.textbooks[0];
  const sections = ((meta.sections[tb] || {})[grade] || []);
  el('sectionSelect').innerHTML = sections.map(sec => `<option>${sec}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderStageOptions() {
  const stages = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  el('stageArea').innerHTML = stages.map(s => `<label class="ck">
<input type="checkbox" data-stage value="${s}" checked> <span>${s}</span>
</label>`).join('');
}

function renderTypeOptions() {
  const types = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].types;
  el('typeArea').innerHTML = types.map(t => `<label class="ck">
<input type="checkbox" data-type value="${t}" checked> <span>${t}</span>
</label>`).join('');
}

function selectedValues(selector) {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector + ':checked')).map(x => x.value);
}

function selectedCategories() {
  const stages = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!stages.length) return [];
  const map = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].stageMap[currentGrade()] || {};
  const set = new Set();
  stages.forEach(stage => (map[stage] || [stage]).forEach(c => set.add(c)));
  return Array.from(set);
}

function passesVocab(item, overrideMode) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return true;
  const mode = overrideMode || (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  if (mode === 'off') return true;
  if (item.grade !== currentGrade()) return true;
  const rec = meta.passMeta[item.id] || {};
  const minIdx = rec[currentTextbook()];
  if (meta.vocabCoordinateVersion !== 'v7-2026-08-18-1based') return false;
  if (minIdx === -2) return true;
  if (!Number.isInteger(minIdx) || minIdx <= 0) return false;
  const selectedOrdinal = currentSectionIndex() + 1;
  return minIdx <= selectedOrdinal;
}

// ---- Mikami prerequisite grammar + runtime quality gates ----
function selectedStageCeiling() {
  if (currentSubject() !== '英語') return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  const selected = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!selected.length) return -1;
  return selected.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, stages.indexOf(s)), -1);
}

function stageIndexByNameContains(...needles) {
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const s = stages[i];
    if (needles.some(n => s.includes(n))) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function targetStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const cfg = subjectConfig['英語'];
  const stages = cfg.stagesByGrade[item.grade] || [];
  const map = cfg.stageMap[item.grade] || {};
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const cats = map[stages[i]] || [stages[i]];
    if (cats.includes(item.category)) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;

function requiredGrammarStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const text = String(item.q || '') + ' ' + String(item.a || '');
  let req = Math.max(0, targetStageIndex(item));
  const bump = i => { if (i >= 0) req = Math.max(req, i); };

  // Detect only structures that can make an otherwise earlier problem depend on a later grammar unit.
  if (/\b(will|won't)\b|\b(am|is|are) going to\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndexByNameContains('未来'));
  if (/\b(was|were)\s+[A-Za-z]+ing\b/i.test(text

--- vocab #19 @ 2586017 ---
ka|She) (?:love|begin|stop)\b/.test(both)) return false;
  if (/Do you have (?:visited|been|finished|lost|lived|studied|seen|done)/i.test(both)) return false;
  if (/Did You ne\b|Do You\b|Do They\b|Does She\b|Does He\b/.test(both)) return false;
  if (/否定文または疑問文/.test(q)) return false;
  if (/日本語で説明しなさい/.test(q) && /^[A-Za-z]/.test(a)) return false;
  if (/\(\s*she\s*\/\s*her\s*\/\s*her\s*\)/i.test(q)) return false;
  if (/\(\s*he\s*\/\s*him\s*\/\s*him\s*\)/i.test(q)) return false;
  if (String(item.id || '').startsWith('M2-INF2-')) {
    if (item.type === '空所補充' && /^to$/i.test(a) && /\bto\s*\(\s*\)\s*(?=[A-Za-z])/i.test(q)) return false;
    if (/^(?:He|She|Yuki|Mika|Takumi|Ken|Emi|Tom|The student|The teacher|This boy|This girl|My mother|My father|My brother|My sister|My friend) (?:want|need|like) to\b/i.test(a)) return false;
  }

  // Target-grammar evidence: do not let a mislabeled vocabulary-only item through.
  if (item.category === '動名詞' && !/[A-Za-z]+ing\b/.test(both)) return false;
  if (item.category === '比較' && !/\bthan\b|\bas\s+\w+\s+as\b|\bmost\b|\w+est\b/i.test(both)) return false;
  const infinitiveBlank = item.type === '空所補充' && /^to$/i.test(a) && /\(\s*\)\s*[A-Za-z]+\b/.test(q);
  if ((item.category === '不定詞' || item.category === '不定詞①' || item.category === '不定詞②') && !/\bto\s+[A-Za-z]+\b/i.test(both) && !infinitiveBlank) return false;
  if (String(item.category).startsWith('現在完了形') && !/\b(?:have|has)\b/i.test(both)) return false;
  if (item.category === '関係代名詞') {
    if (!/\b(?:who|which|that)\b/i.test(both)) return false;
    if (item.type === '空所補充' && /\(\s*\)/.test(q)) {
      if (!/who\s*\/\s*which/i.test(q)) return false;
      if (!/^(?:who|which)$/i.test(a)) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

}

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function refreshList() {
  const subj = el('listSubject').value;
  const grade = el('listGrade').value;
  const limit = parseInt(el('listLimit').value, 10);
  const kw = el('listKeyword').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const vocabMode = el('listVocabMode').value === 'off' ? 'off' : (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  let rows = qb.filter(item => {
    if (subj !== 'all' && item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (grade !== 'all' && item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (item.subject === '英語' && item.grade === currentGrade() && !passesVocab(item, vocabMode)) return false;
    if (kw) {
      const hay = (item.id + ' ' + item.category + ' ' + item.type + ' ' + item.q + ' ' + item.a).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(kw)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  el('listStatus').textContent = '一覧 ' + rows.length + '件';
  el('listStatus').classList.toggle('good', rows.length > 0);
  rows = rows.slice(0, limit);
  el('listBody').innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <tr>
<td>${item.id}</td>
<td>${item.subject}</td>
<td>${item.grade}</td>
<td>${item.category}</td>
<td>${item.type}</td>
<td>${item.q}</td>
<td>${item.a}</td>
</tr>
  `).join('');
}

  el('listSubject').value = currentSubject();
  el('listGrade').value = currentGrade();
}

  if (String(item.id || '').startsWith('M2-INF2-')) {
    if (item.type === '空所補充' && /^to$/i.test(a) && /\bto\s*\(\s*\)\s*(?=[A-Za-z])/i.test(q)) return false;
    if (/^(?:He|She|Yuki|Mika|Takumi|Ken|Emi|Tom|The student|The teacher|This boy|This girl|My mother|My father|My brother|My sister|My friend) (?:want|need|like) to\b/i.test(a)) return false;

  // Target-grammar evidence: do not let a mislabeled vocabulary-only item through.
  if (item.category === '動名詞' && !/[A-Za-z]+ing\b/.test(both)) return false;
  if (item.category === '比較' && !/\bthan\b|\bas\s+\w+\s+as\b|\bmost\b|\w+est\b/i.test(both)) return false;

      if (!/who\s*\/\s*which/i.test(q)) return false;
      if (!/^(?:who|which)$/i.test(a)) return false;

  return true;

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

  const grade = currentGrade();

  stages.forEach(stage => (map[stage] || [stage]).forEach(c => set.add(c)));
  return Array.from(set);
}

function passesVocab(item, overrideMode) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return true;
  const mode = overrideMode || (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  if (mode === 'off') return true;
  if (item.grade !== currentGrade()) return true;
  const rec = meta.passMeta[item.id] || {};
  const minIdx = rec[currentTextbook()];
  if (meta.vocabCoordinateVersion !== 'v7-2026-08-18-1based') return false;
  if (minIdx === -2) return true;
  if (!Number.isInteger(minIdx) || minIdx <= 0) return false;
  const selectedOrdinal = currentSectionIndex() + 1;
  return minIdx <= selectedOrdinal;
}

// ---- Mikami prerequisite grammar + runtime quality gates ----
function selectedStageCeiling() {
  if (currentSubject() !== '英語') return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  const selected = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!selected.length) return -1;
  return selected.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, stages.indexOf(s)), -1);
}

function stageIndexByNameContains(...needles) {
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const s = stages[i];
    if (needles.some(n => s.includes(n))) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function targetStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const cfg = subjectConfig['英語'];
  const stages = cfg.stagesByGrade[item.grade] || [];
  const map = cfg.stageMap[item.grade] || {};
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const cats = map[stages[i]] || [stages[i]];
    if (cats.includes(item.category)) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function passesPrereqGrammar(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return true;
  const ceiling = selectedStageCeiling();
  if (ceiling < 0) return false;
  const required = requiredGrammarStageIndex(item);
  return required < 0 || required <= ceiling;
}

function passesQualityGate(item) {
  if (!item || !String(item.q || '').trim() || !String(item.a || '').trim()) return false;
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return true;
  const q = String(item.q || '');
  const a = String(item.a || '');
  const both = q + ' ' + a;

  // Known invalid / non-unique generation families.
  if (/This is (?:he|she|we)\b/i.test(both)) return false;
  if (/\bthan He\b/.test(both)) return false;
  if (/\b(?:Mika|She) (?:love|begin|stop)\b/.test(both)) return false;

  if (/否定文または疑問文/.test(q)) return false;
  if (/日本語で説明しなさい/.test(q) && /^[A-Za-z]/.test(a)) return false;
  if (/\(\s*she\s*\/\s*her\s*\/\s*her\s*\)/i.test(q)) return false;
  if (/\(\s*he\s*\/\s*him\s*\/\s*him\s*\)/i.test(q)) return false;
  if (String(item.id || '').startsWith('M2-INF2-')) {
    if (item.type === '空所補充' && /^to$/i.test(a) && /\bto\s*\(\s*\)\s*(?=[A-Za-z])/i.test(q)) return false;
    if (/^(?:He|She|Yuki|Mika|Takumi|Ken|Emi|Tom|The student|The teacher|This boy|This girl|My mother|My father|My brother|My sister|My friend) (?:want|need|like) to\b/i.test(a)) return false;
  }

  // Target-grammar evidence: do not let a mislabeled vocabulary-only item through.
  if (item.category === '動名詞' && !/[A-Za-z]+ing\b/.test(both)) return false;

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

  const escapeHtml = value => String(v

let currentQuestions = [];
let showAnswers = false;
const STORAGE_KEY = 'mikamijuku_problem_app_v3';

function captureState() {
  return {
    subject: el('subjectSelect')?.value || '英語',
    grade: el('gradeSelect')?.value || '中1',
    textbook: el('textbookSelect')?.value || '',
    section: el('sectionSelect')?.value || '',
    questionCount: el('questionCount')?.value || '10',
    vocabMode: el('vocabMode')?.value || 'on',
    pickMode: el('pickMode')?.value || 'random',
    stages: selectedValues('input[data-stage]'),
    types: selectedValues('input[data-type]'),
    listSubject: el('listSubject')?.value || '英語',
    listGrade: el('listGrade')?.value || 'all',
    listLimit: el('listLimit')?.value || '50',
    listKeyword: el('listKeyword')?.value || '',
    listVocabMode: el('listVocabMode')?.value || 'same',
    currentQuestionIds: currentQuestions.map(q => q.id),
    showAnswers
  };
}

function saveState(silent = true) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(captureState()));
    if (!silent && el('globalSaveStatus')) {
      el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = 'この端末に保存しました。';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存に失敗しました。';
  }
}

function restoreQuestionSetByIds(ids) {
  const map = new Map(qb.map(q => [q.id, q]));
  const restored = (ids || []).map(id => map.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (restored.length) {
    currentQuestions = restored;
    el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = restored.map((q, i) => `
      <div class="q">
        <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
        <div>${q.q}</div>
        <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    el('builderStatus').textContent = '保存されていた問題セットを復元しました。';
    el('builderStatus').classList.add('good');
  }
}

  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;

--- textbook #10 @ 2576534 ---

function safeSetValue(id, value) {
  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;
  const has = Array.from(node.options || []).some(op => op.value === value || op.textContent === value);
  if (has) node.value = value;
}

function setCheckedValues(selector, values) {
  const set = new Set(values || []);
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(ch => {
    ch.checked = set.size ? set.has(ch.value) : true;
  });
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);
  renderSectionOptions();
  renderStageOptions();
  renderTypeOptions();
  safeSetValue('textbookSelect', state.textbook);
  renderSectionOptions();
  safeSetValue('sectionSelect', state.section);
  safeSetValue('questionCount', state.questionCount);
  safeSetValue('vocabMode', state.vocabMode);
  safeSetValue('pickMode', state.pickMode);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-stage]', state.stages);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-type]', state.types);
  syncListSelectors();
  safeSetValue('listSubject', state.listSubject);
  safeSetValue('listGrade', state.listGrade);
  safeSetValue('listLimit', state.listLimit);
  safeSetValue('listVocabMode', state.listVocabMode);
  if (el('listKeyword')) el('listKeyword').value = state.listKeyword || '';
  showAnswers = !!state.showAnswers;
  restoreQuestionSetByIds(state.currentQuestionIds || []);
  updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
}

function exportStateFile() {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(captureState(), null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = 'mikamijuku_app_state_latest.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 3000);
  if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを書き出しました。';
}

function importStateFile(file) {
  const fr = new FileReader();
  fr.onload = () => {
    try {
      const state = JSON.parse(fr.result);
      applyState(state);
      saveState(true);
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを読み込みました。';
    } catch (e) {
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データの読み込みに失敗しました。';
    }
  };
  fr.readAsText(file, 'utf-8');
}

const currentSubject = () => el('subjectSelect').value;
const currentGrade = () => el('gradeSelect').value;
const currentTextbook = () => el('textbookSelect').value;
const currentSectionIndex = () => Math.max(0, el('sectionSelect').selectedIndex);
const useVocabGate = () => el('vocabMode').value === 'on';

function renderTabs() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const target = document.getElementById('screen-' + btn.dataset.screen);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
}

function renderTextbookOptions() {
  el('textbookSelect').innerHTML = meta.textbooks.map(tb => `<option>${tb}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderSectionOptions() {
  const subject = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade().replace('中','');
  if (subject !== '英語') {
    el('textbookWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    el('sectionWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  el('textbookWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  el('sectionWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  const tb = currentTextbook() || meta.textbooks[0];
  const sections = ((meta.sections[tb] || {})[grade] || []);
  el('sectionSelect').innerHTML = sections.map(sec => `<option>${sec}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderStageOptions() {
  const stages = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  el('stageArea').innerHTML = stages.map(s => `<label class="ck">
<input type="checkbox" data-stage value="${s}" checked> <span>${s}</span>
</label>`).join('');
}

  const types = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].types;
  el('typeArea').innerHTML = types.map(t => `<label class="ck">

// ---- Mikami prerequisite grammar + runtime quality gates ----
function selectedStageCeiling() {
  if (currentSubject() !== '英語') return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  const selected = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!selected.length) return -1;
  return selected.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, stages.indexOf(s)), -1);
}

function stageIndexByNameContains(...needles) {
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const s = stages[i];
    if (needles.some(n => s.includes(n))) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function targetStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const cfg = subjectConfig['英語'];
  const stages = cfg.stagesByGrade[item.grade] || [];
  const map = cfg.stageMap[item.grade] || {};
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const cats = map[stages[i]] || [stages[i]];
    if (cats.includes(item.category)) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function requiredGrammarStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const text = String(item.q || '') + ' ' + String(item.a || '');
  let req = Math.max(0, targetStageIndex(item));
  const bump = i => { if (i >= 0) req = Math.max(req, i); };

  // Detect only structures that can make an otherwise earlier problem depend on a later grammar unit.
  if (/\b(will|won't)\b|\b(am|is|are) going to\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndexByNameContains('未来'));
  if (/\b(was|were)\s+[A-Za

--- textbook #22 @ 2588108 ---
s.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function printQuestions() {
  if (!currentQuestions.length) return;
  const escapeHtml = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[char]));
  const renderQuestion = (q, index) => `<div class="q">
<div class="n">Q${index + 1}　${escapeHtml(q.id)}</div>
<div class="body">${escapeHtml(q.q)}</div>${showAnswers ? `<div class="a">答え: ${escapeHtml(q.a)}</div>` : '<div class="answer-space" aria-label="解答欄">
</div>'}</div>`;
  const pages = [];
  for (let startIndex = 0; startIndex < currentQuestions.length; startIndex += 10) {
    const pageQuestions = currentQuestions.slice(startIndex, startIndex + 10);
    pages.push({
      html: pageQuestions.map((q, offset) => renderQuestion(q, startIndex + offset)).join(''),
      rows:

  .q{display:flex;flex-direction:column;break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid;border:0.2mm solid #c7c7c7;border-radius:1.5mm;padding:2.5mm;margin:0;min-height:0}
  .n{font-weight:700;font-size:8pt;margin-bottom:1mm}.body{white-space:pre-wrap}.a{margin-top:2mm;color:#075c4c;font-size:8pt}
  .answer-space{flex:1;min-height:18mm;margin-top:2mm;background:repeating-linear-gradient(to bottom,transparent 0,transparent 7.2mm,#a8a8a8 7.4mm,transparent 7.6mm)}

  const w = window.open('', '_blank');

  w.document.open();
  w.document.write(printHtml);
  w.document.close();
}

function refreshList() {
  const subj = el('listSubject').value;
  const grade = el('listGrade').value;
  const limit = parseInt(el('listLimit').value, 10);
  const kw = el('listKeyword').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const vocabMode = el('listVocabMode').value === 'off' ? 'off' : (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  let rows = qb.filter(item => {
    if (subj !== 'all' && item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (grade !== 'all' && item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (item.subject === '英語' && item.grade === currentGrade() && !passesVocab(item, vocabMode)) return false;
    if (kw) {
      const hay = (item.id + ' ' + item.category + ' ' + item.type + ' ' + item.q + ' ' + item.a).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(kw)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  el('listStatus').textContent = '一覧 ' + rows.length + '件';
  el('listStatus').classList.toggle('good', rows.length > 0);
  rows = rows.slice(0, limit);
  el('listBody').innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <tr>
<td>${item.id}</td>
<td>${item.subject}</td>
<td>${item.grade}</td>
<td>${item.category}</td>
<td>${item.type}</td>
<td>${item.q}</td>
<td>${item.a}</td>
</tr>
  `).join('');
}

function syncListSelectors() {
  el('listSubject').value = currentSubject();
  el('listGrade').value = currentGrade();
}

function wireEvents() {
  renderTabs();
  el('subjectSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); renderStageOptions(); renderTypeOptions(); syncListSelectors(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('gradeSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); renderStageOptions(); renderTypeOptions(); syncListSelectors(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('textbookSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('sectionSelect').addEventListener('change', () => updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()));
  el('vocabMode').addEventListener('change', () => updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()));
  document.addEventListener('change', evt => {
    if (evt.target.matches('input[data-stage], input[data-type]')) updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
  });
  el('generateBtn').addEventListener('click', renderQuestions);
  el('toggleAnswersBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { showAnswers = !showAnswers; renderQuestions(); });
  el('printBtn').addEventListener('click', printQuestions);
  el('toListBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { document.querySelector('.tab[data-screen="list"]').click(); refreshList(); saveState(true); });
  el('refreshListBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { refreshList(); saveState(true); });
  const autoSave = () => saveState(true);
  document.addEventListener('change', evt => {
    if (evt.target.closest('select,input,textarea')) autoSave();
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', evt => {
    if (evt.target.closest('input[type=text], textarea')) autoSave();
  });
  if (el('exportJsonBtn')) el('exportJsonBtn').addEventListener('click', exportStateFile);
  if (el('importJsonBtn')) el('importJsonBtn').addEventListener('click', () => el('importJsonInput').click());
  if (el('importJsonInput')) el('importJsonInput').addEventListener('change', evt => {
    const file = evt.target.files && evt.target.files[0];
    if (file) importStateFile(file);
    evt.target.value = '';
  });
}

let currentQuestions = [];
let showAnswers = false;
const STORAGE_KEY = 'mikamijuku_problem_app_v3';

function captureState() {
  return {
    subject: el('subjectSelect')?.value || '英語',
    grade: el('gradeSelect')?.value || '中1',
    textbook: el('textbookSelect')?.value || '',
    section: el('sectionSelect')?.value || '',
    questionCount: el('questionCount')?.value || '10',
    vocabMode: el('vocabMode')?.value || 'on',
    pickMode: el('pickMode')?.value || 'random',
    stages: selectedValues('input[data-stage]'),
    types: selectedValues('input[data-type]'),
    listSubject: el('listSubject')?.value || '英語',
    listGrade: el('listGrade')?.value || 'all',
    listLimit: el('listLimit')?.value || '50',
    listKeyword: el('listKeyword')?.value || '',
    listVocabMode: el('listVocabMode')?.value || 'same',
    currentQuestionIds: currentQuestions.map(q => q.id),
    showAnswers
  };
}

function saveState(silent = true) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(captureState()));
    if (!silent && el('globalSaveStatus')) {
      el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = 'この端末に保存しました。';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存に失敗しました。';
  }
}

function restoreQuestionSetByIds(ids) {
  const map = new Map(qb.map(q => [q.id, q]));
  const restored = (ids || []).map(id => map.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (restored.length) {
    currentQuestions = restored;
    el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = restored.map((q, i) => `
      <div class="q">
        <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
        <div>${q.q}</div>
        <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    el('builderStatus').textContent = '保存されていた問題セットを復元しました。';
    el('builderStatus').classList.add('good');
  }
}

function safeSetValue(id, value) {
  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;
  const has = Array.from(node.options || []).some(op => op.value === v

    if (!silent && el('globalSaveStatus')) {
      el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = 'この端末に保存しました。';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存に失敗しました。';
  }
}

function restoreQuestionSetByIds(ids) {
  const map = new Map(qb.map(q => [q.id, q]));
  const restored = (ids || []).map(id => map.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (restored.length) {
    currentQuestions = restored;
    el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = restored.map((q, i) => `
      <div class="q">
        <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
        <div>${q.q}</div>
        <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    el('builderStatus').textContent = '保存されていた問題セットを復元しました。';
    el('builderStatus').classList.add('good');
  }
}

function safeSetValue(id, value) {
  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;
  const has = Array.from(node.options || []).some(op => op.value === value || op.textContent === value);
  if (has) node.value = value;
}

function setCheckedValues(selector, values) {
  const set = new Set(values || []);
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(ch => {
    ch.checked = set.size ? set.has(ch.value) : true;
  });
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);
  renderSectionOptions();
  renderStageOptions();
  renderTypeOptions();
  safeSetValue('textbookSelect', state.textbook);
  renderSectionOptions();
  safeSetValue('sectionSelect', state.section);
  safeSetValue('questionCount', state.questionCount);
  safeSetValue('vocabMode', state.vocabMode);
  safeSetValue('pickMode', state.pickMode);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-stage]', state.stages);
  setCheckedValues('input[data-type]', state.types);
  syncListSelectors();
  safeSetValue('listSubject', state.listSubject);
  safeSetValue('listGrade', state.listGrade);
  safeSetValue('listLimit', state.listLimit);
  safeSetValue('listVocabMode', state.listVocabMode);
  if (el('listKeyword')) el('listKeyword').value = state.listKeyword || '';
  showAnswers = !!state.showAnswers;
  restoreQuestionSetByIds(state.currentQuestionIds || []);
  updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
}

    }
  };

}

function renderTabs() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const target = document.getElementById('screen-' + btn.dataset.screen);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
}

function renderTextbookOptions() {
  el('textbookSelect').innerHTML = meta.textbooks.map(tb => `<option>${tb}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderSectionOptions() {
  const subject = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade().replace('中','');
  if (subject !== '英語') {
    el('textbookWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    el('sectionWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  el('textbookWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  el('sectionWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  const tb = currentTextbook() || meta.textbooks[0];
  const sections = ((meta.sections[tb] || {})[grade] || []);
  el('sectionSelect').innerHTML = sections.map(sec => `<option>${sec}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderStageOptions() {
  const stages = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  el('stageArea').innerHTML = stages.map(s => `<label class="ck">
<input type="checkbox" data-stage value="${s}" checked> <span>${s}</span>
</label>`).join('');
}

function selectedValues(selector) {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(selector + ':checked')).map(x => x.value);
}

function selectedCategories() {
  const stages = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!stages.length) return [];
  const map = subjectConfig[currentSubject()].stageMap[currentGrade()] || {};
  const set = new Set();
  stages.forEach(stage => (map[stage] || [stage]).forEach(c => set.add(c)));
  return Array.from(set);
}

      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const target = document.getElementById('screen-' + btn.dataset.screen);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
}

  el('textbookSelect').innerHTML = meta.textbooks.map(tb => `<option>${tb}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderSectionOptions() {
  const subject = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade().replace('中','');
  if (subject !== '英語') {
    el('textbookWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    el('sectionWrap').classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  el('textbookWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  el('sectionWrap').classList.remove('hidden');
  const tb = currentTextbook() || meta.textbooks[0];
  const sections = ((meta.sections[tb] || {})[grade] || []);
  el('sectionSelect').innerHTML = sections.map(sec => `<option>${sec}</option>`).join('');
}

// ---- Mikami prerequisite grammar + runtime quality gates ----
function selectedStageCeiling() {
  if (currentSubject() !== '英語') return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  const selected = selectedValues('input[data-stage]');
  if (!selected.length) return -1;
  return selected.reduce((max, s) => Math.max(max, stages.indexOf(s)), -1);
}

function stageIndexByNameContains(...needles) {
  const stages = subjectConfig['英語'].stagesByGrade[currentGrade()] || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const s = stages[i];
    if (needles.some(n => s.includes(n))) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function targetStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const cfg = subjectConfig['英語'];
  const stages = cfg.stagesByGrade[item.grade] || [];
  const map = cfg.stageMap[item.grade] || {};
  for (let i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
    const cats = map[stages[i]] || [stages[i]];
    if (cats.includes(item.category)) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function requiredGrammarStageIndex(item) {
  if (item.subject !== '英語') return -1;
  const text = String(item.q || '') + ' ' + String(item.a || '');
  let req = Math.max(0, targetStageIndex(item));
  const bump = i => { if (i >= 0) req = Math.max(req, i); };

  // Detect only structures that can make an otherwise earlier problem depend on a later grammar unit.
  if (/\b(will|won't)\b|\b(am|is|are) going to\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndexByNameContains('未来'));
  if (/\b(was|were)\s+[A-Za-z]+ing\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndexByNameContains('過去進行'));
  if (/\b(am|is|are)\s+[A-Za-z]+ing\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndexByNameContains('進行形'));
  if (/\b(can|must|may|should)\b/i.test(text)) bump(stageIndex

--- section #21 @ 2588143 ---
em.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function printQuestions() {
  if (!currentQuestions.length) return;
  const escapeHtml = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[char]));
  const renderQuestion = (q, index) => `<div class="q">
<div class="n">Q${index + 1}　${escapeHtml(q.id)}</div>
<div class="body">${escapeHtml(q.q)}</div>${showAnswers ? `<div class="a">答え: ${escapeHtml(q.a)}</div>` : '<div class="answer-space" aria-label="解答欄">
</div>'}</div>`;
  const pages = [];
  for (let startIndex = 0; startIndex < currentQuestions.length; startIndex += 10) {
    const pageQuestions = currentQuestions.slice(startIndex, startIndex + 10);
    pages.push({
      html: pageQuestions.map((q, offset) => renderQuestion(q, startIndex + offset)).join(''),
      rows: Math.ceil(pageQuestions.length / 2

function refreshList() {
  const subj = el('listSubject').value;
  const grade = el('listGrade').value;
  const limit = parseInt(el('listLimit').value, 10);
  const kw = el('listKeyword').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const vocabMode = el('listVocabMode').value === 'off' ? 'off' : (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  let rows = qb.filter(item => {
    if (subj !== 'all' && item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (grade !== 'all' && item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (item.subject === '英語' && item.grade === currentGrade() && !passesVocab(item, vocabMode)) return false;
    if (kw) {
      const hay = (item.id + ' ' + item.category + ' ' + item.type + ' ' + item.q + ' ' + item.a).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(kw)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  el('listStatus').textContent = '一覧 ' + rows.length + '件';
  el('listStatus').classList.toggle('good', rows.length > 0);
  rows = rows.slice(0, limit);
  el('listBody').innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <tr>
<td>${item.id}</td>
<td>${item.subject}</td>
<td>${item.grade}</td>
<td>${item.category}</td>
<td>${item.type}</td>
<td>${item.q}</td>
<td>${item.a}</td>
</tr>
  `).join('');
}

function refreshList() {
  const subj = el('listSubject').value;
  const grade = el('listGrade').value;
  const limit = parseInt(el('listLimit').value, 10);
  const kw = el('listKeyword').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const vocabMode = el('listVocabMode').value === 'off' ? 'off' : (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  let rows = qb.filter(item => {
    if (subj !== 'all' && item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (grade !== 'all' && item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (item.subject === '英語' && item.grade === currentGrade() && !passesVocab(item, vocabMode)) return false;
    if (kw) {
      const hay = (item.id + ' ' + item.category + ' ' + item.type + ' ' + item.q + ' ' + item.a).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(kw)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  el('listStatus').textContent = '一覧 ' + rows.length + '件';
  el('listStatus').classList.toggle('good', rows.length > 0);
  rows = rows.slice(0, limit);
  el('listBody').innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <tr>
<td>${item.id}</td>
<td>${item.subject}</td>
<td>${item.grade}</td>
<td>${item.category}</td>
<td>${item.type}</td>
<td>${item.q}</td>
<td>${item.a}</td>
</tr>
  `).join('');
}

function saveState(silent = true) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(captureState()));
    if (!silent && el('globalSaveStatus')) {
      el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = 'この端末に保存しました。';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存に失敗しました。';
  }
}

function restoreQuestionSetByIds(ids) {
  const map = new Map(qb.map(q => [q.id, q]));
  const restored = (ids || []).map(id => map.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (restored.length) {
    currentQuestions = restored;
    el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = restored.map((q, i) => `
      <div class="q">
        <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
        <div>${q.q}</div>
        <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
      </div>
    `).join('');
    el('builderStatus').textContent = '保存されていた問題セットを復元しました。';
    el('builderStatus').classList.add('good');
  }
}

function safeSetValue(id, value) {
  const node = el(id);
  if (!node || value == null) return;
  const has = Array.from(node.options || []).some(op => op.value === value || op.textContent === value);
  if (has) node.value = value;
}

function setCheckedValues(selector, values) {
  const set = new Set(values || []);
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(ch => {
    ch.checked = set.size ? set.has(ch.value) : true;
  });
}

function applyState(state) {
  if (!state) return;
  safeSetValue('subjectSelect', state.subject);
  safeSetValue('gradeSelect', state.grade);

  renderStageOptions();

function exportStateFile() {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(captureState(), null, 2)], {type:'application/json'});
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = 'mikamijuku_app_state_latest.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 3000);
  if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを書き出しました。';
}

function importStateFile(file) {
  const fr = new FileReader();
  fr.onload = () => {
    try {
      const state = JSON.parse(fr.result);
      applyState(state);
      saveState(true);
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データを読み込みました。';
    } catch (e) {
      if (el('globalSaveStatus')) el('globalSaveStatus').textContent = '保存データの読み込みに失敗しました。';
    }
  };
  fr.readAsText(file, 'utf-8');
}

const currentSubject = () => el('subjectSelect').value;
const currentGrade = () => el('gradeSelect').value;
const currentTextbook = () => el('textbookSelect').value;
const currentSectionIndex = () => Math.max(0, el('sectionSelect').selectedIndex);
const useVocabGate = () => el('vocabMode').value === 'on';

function renderTabs() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
      btn.classList.add('active');
      const target = document.getElementById('screen-' + btn.dataset.screen);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
    });
  });
}

  if (item.category === '比較' && !/\bthan\b|\bas\s+\w+\s+as\b|\bmost\b|\w+est\b/i.test(both)) return false;
  const infinitiveBlank = item.type === '空所補充' && /^to$/i.test(a) && /\(\s*\)\s*[A-Za-z]+\b/.test(q);
  if ((item.category === '不定詞' || item.category === '不定詞①' || item.category === '不定詞②') && !/\bto\s+[A-Za-z]+\b/i.test(both) && !infinitiveBlank) return false;
  if (String(item.category).startsWith('現在完了形') && !/\b(?:have|has)\b/i.test(both)) return false;
  if (item.category === '関係代名詞') {
    if (!/\b(?:who|which|that)\b/i.test(both)) return false;
    if (item.type === '空所補充' && /\(\s*\)/.test(q)) {
      if (!/who\s*\/\s*which/i.test(q)) return false;
      if (!/^(?:who|which)$/i.test(a)) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function printQuestions() {
  if (!currentQuestions.length) return;
  const escapeHtml = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[char]));
  const renderQuestion = (q, index) => `<div class="q">
<div class="n">Q${index + 1}　${escapeHtml(q.id)}</div>
<div class="body">${escapeHtml(q.q)}</div>${showAnswers ? `<div class="a">答え: ${escapeHtml(q.a)}</div>` : '<div class="answer-space" aria-label="解答欄">
</div>'}</div>`;
  const pages = [];
  for (let startIndex = 0; startIndex < currentQuestions.length; startIndex += 10) {
    const pageQuestions = currentQuestions.slice(startIndex, startIndex + 10);
    pages.push({
      html: pageQuestions.map((q, offset) => renderQuestion(q, startIndex + offset)).join(''),
      rows: Math.ceil(pageQuestions.length / 2)
    });
  }
  const printHtml = `<!doctype html>
<html lang="ja">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<title>問題プリント</title>
<style>
  @page{size:A4 portrait;margin:8mm}
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI","Yu Gothic",sans-serif;margin:0;color:#111;font-size:

--- filter #7 @ 2592168 ---
)} ${currentSubject()} 問題プリント</h2>
<div class="meta">教科書: ${currentSubject()==='英語' ? escapeHtml(currentTextbook()) : '—'} / 単語はここまで: ${currentSubject()==='英語' ? escapeHtml(el('sectionSelect').value || '—') : '—'} / ${pageIndex + 1}頁</div>
<div class="questions" style="grid-template-rows:repeat(${page.rows}, minmax(0, 1fr))">${page.html}</div>
</section>`).join('')}
  <script>window.onload=()=>window.print()<\/script>
</body>
</html>`;
  const w = window.open('', '_blank');
  if (!w) return;
  w.document.open();
  w.document.write(printHtml);
  w.document.close();
}

function refreshList() {
  const subj = el('listSubject').value;
  const grade = el('listGrade').value;
  const limit = parseInt(el('listLimit').value, 10);
  const kw = el('listKeyword').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const vocabMode = el('listVocabMode').value === 'off' ? 'off' : (useVocabGate() ? 'on' : 'off');
  let rows = qb.filter(item => {
    if (subj !== 'all' && item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (grade !== 'all' && item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (item.subject === '英語' && item.grade === currentGrade() && !passesVocab(item, vocabMode)) return false;
    if (kw) {
      const hay = (item.id + ' ' + item.category + ' ' + item.type + ' ' + item.q + ' ' + item.a).toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(kw)) return false;
    }
    return true;
  });
  el('listStatus').textContent = '一覧 ' + rows.length + '件';
  el('listStatus').classList.toggle('good', rows.length > 0);
  rows = rows.slice(0, limit);
  el('listBody').innerHTML = rows.map(item => `
    <tr>
<td>${item.id}</td>
<td>${item.subject}</td>
<td>${item.grade}</td>
<td>${item.category}</td>
<td>${item.type}</td>
<td>${item.q}</td>
<td>${item.a}</td>
</tr>
  `).join('');
}

function syncListSelectors() {
  el('listSubject').value = currentSubject();
  el('listGrade').value = currentGrade();
}

  el('subjectSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); renderStageOptions(); renderTypeOptions(); syncListSelectors(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('gradeSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); renderStageOptions(); renderTypeOptions(); syncListSelectors(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('textbookSelect').addEventListener('change', () => { renderSectionOptions(); updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()); });
  el('sectionSelect').addEventListener('change', () => updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()));
  el('vocabMode').addEventListener('change', () => updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered()));
  document.addEventListener('change', evt => {
    if (evt.target.matches('input[data-stage], input[data-type]')) updateBuilderStatus(baseFiltered());
  });
  el('generateBtn').addEventListener('click', renderQuestions);
  el('toggleAnswersBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { showAnswers = !showAnswers; renderQuestions(); });
  el('printBtn').addEventListener('click', printQuestions);
  el('toListBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { document.querySelector('.tab[data-screen="list"]').click(); refreshList(); saveState(true); });
  el('refreshListBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { refreshList(); saveState(true); });
  const autoSave = () => saveState(true);
  document.addEventListener('change', evt => {
    if (evt.target.closest('select,input,textarea')) autoSave();
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', evt => {
    if (evt.target.closest('input[type=text], textarea')) autoSave();
  });
  if (el('exportJsonBtn')) el('exportJsonBtn').addEventListener('click', exportStateFile);
  if (el('importJsonBtn')) el('importJsonBtn').addEventListener('click', () => el('importJsonInput').click());
  if (el('importJsonInput')) el('importJsonInput').addEventListener('change', evt => {
    const file = evt.target.files && evt.target.files[0];
    if (file) importStateFile(file);
    evt.target.value = '';
  });
}

function syncListSelectors() {
  el('listSubject').value = currentSubject();
  el('listGrade').value = currentGrade();
}

function baseFiltered(overrideMode) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const categories = selectedCategories();
  const types = selectedValues('input[data-type]');
  return qb.filter(item => {
    if (item.subject !== subj) return false;
    if (item.grade !== grade) return false;
    if (categories.length && !categories.includes(item.category)) return false;
    if (types.length && !types.includes(item.type)) return false;
    if (!passesVocab(item, overrideMode)) return false;
    if (!passesPrereqGrammar(item)) return false;
    if (!passesQualityGate(item)) return false;
    return true;
  });
}

function shuffle(arr) {
  const a = arr.slice();
  for (let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i+1));
    const tmp = a[i]; a[i] = a[j]; a[j] = tmp;
  }
  return a;
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

function updateBuilderStatus(candidates) {
  const subj = currentSubject();
  const grade = currentGrade();
  const sameGradeCount = qb.filter(x => x.subject === subj && x.grade === grade).length;
  let msg = '全問題 ' + meta.counts.all + '件 / ' + grade + 'の' + subj + '元データ ' + sameGradeCount + '件 / この条件で使える候補 ' + candidates.length + '件';
  if (subj === '英語') {
    msg += ' / 教科書: ' + currentTextbook() + ' / 単語はここまで: ' + (el('sectionSelect').value || '未選択');
  }
  el('builderStatus').textContent = msg;
  el('builderStatus').classList.toggle('good', candidates.length > 0);
}

function renderQuestions() {
  const count = parseInt(el('questionCount').value, 10);
  const pickMode = el('pickMode').value;
  const candidates = baseFiltered();
  updateBuilderStatus(candidates);
  if (!candidates.length) {
    currentQuestions = [];
    el('questionPreview').innerHTML = 'この条件では問題が見つかりませんでした。単語制限を外すか、範囲や問題タイプを広げてください。';
    return;
  }
  const picked = (pickMode === 'random' ? shuffle(candidates) : candidates).slice(0, count);
  currentQuestions = picked;
  el('questionPreview').classList.toggle('showAnswers', showAnswers);
  el('questionPreview').innerHTML = picked.map((q, i) => `
    <div class="q">
      <div class="qnum">Q${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}　${q.id}　/　${q.category}　/　${q.type}</div>
      <div>${q.q}</div>
      <div class="ans">答え: ${q.a}</div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}
